The exact creator South African Soulful Deep House playlist includes Academic Deep11 and Beat Soul23, both positively named in the selected mix; Tiyani himself is not asserted a creator member. His own recurring podcast expressly curates this South African deep-and-soulful community, and issuer STORK independently describes the national soulful house producer network, emotional/melodic textures, collaborative releases and DJ circulation. Existing south_african_deep_house remains a broad regional deep-house field already including soulful/jazzy/Afro-house practices, not merely Jazzuelle's one programme. Existing south_african_house also explicitly includes gospel-inflected voices, Rhodes/pads and deep chords. The proposed category is a narrower soulful curatorial/production branch within those overlapping fields, not a claim of exclusion, a new nationality or a distinct instrument. Exact creator playlist includes Street Academics23, The Imbachi6 and Earthgrime46; this bridges the chosen crew without calling every guest Malayali or every verse Malayalam. Full desi_hip_hop already spans South Asian languages and diaspora, so this is its overlapping language-community branch, not a claim that desi hip-hop means only Hindi or DIVINE. Desi trap is a narrower production style and does not exhaust Malayalam rap; Malayalam pop and indie are broader language song/independent fields, whose selected band palettes do not restrict their definitions. Mappila paattu names a historically rooted Arabi-Malayalam song literature and religious/social practices, not all Kerala hip-hop.
